> **Filename convention:** Stories live at `.factory/stories/S-N.MM-<short-description>.md`. Example: S-1.05 lives at `S-1.05-wasmtime-integration.md`.

> **Cycle field semantics:** Each story's `cycle:` frontmatter records the cycle that ORIGINALLY CREATED that story. For the 22 merged stories migrated in Phase 1.8 (S-N.M -> S-N.MM), `cycle: v1.0.0-greenfield` is preserved as immutable history. The current cycle (`v1.0-brownfield-backfill`) is for backfill of formal specs around already-shipped stories, not for new story creation.

> **S-7.03 delivery:** PR #13 merged to develop at 4db2340 on 2026-04-26. 18/18 bats tests GREEN. Worktree feat/tdd-discipline-hardening (9b1624b → 121d24c, 9 commits). Spec convergence: 17 adversarial passes.
> **S-7.04 filed 2026-05-04:** Process-gap from research investigation (PR #78 context). Before marking a story shipped, every AC must be linked to a specific test name + test file path + test result; the implementer agent MUST refuse to mark complete without this artifact. Specifically targets integration ACs ("X is wired into Y") where unit tests pass but end-to-end path is unverified. Motivated by S-3.04 AC-001 false-shipped discovery (Router::submit unwired in main.rs since April 24, 2026). See LESSON-2026-05-04-001 in cycles/v1.0-brownfield-backfill/lessons.md. Target: v1.0.1. **discipline_scope: universal** — the AC-test-link discipline is part of the per-story-delivery flow that vsdd-factory provides to ALL managed projects; implementation lives in the vsdd-factory plugin's hooks/agents, not in project-specific config.
> **S-7.05 filed 2026-05-04:** Process-gap from forensic field inventory (PR #78 context). CI lint hook `validate-dashboard-fields-against-emitters.sh` greps Grafana JSON files for event_type= and field references, cross-checks against WASM plugin host::emit_event call sites + bash hook emit-event invocations + dispatcher lifecycle constants, fails CI if any dashboard query field has no emitter. Motivated by factory-prs.json querying pr.opened (plugin emits pr.created) and open_to_merge_seconds (never emitted). See LESSON-2026-05-04-002 in cycles/v1.0-brownfield-backfill/lessons.md. Target: v1.0.1. Rides TD-014 emitter-contract awareness. **discipline_scope: vsdd-factory-self** — this lint hook is specific to vsdd-factory's own observability stack and dashboard-emitter contract, not universally applicable.

See `cycles/v1.0-brownfield-backfill/lessons.md` `L-BB-D1201-transient-status-test-doc-comment-recurring-4x-escalation` for the full 4th-recurrence lesson) |

Recommended remedy: amend the BC-5.39.001 convergence-protocol documentation (`vsdd-factory:adversarial-review` skill + the orchestrator's per-cluster/per-story adversary-dispatch prompt) to REQUIRE at least one cross-vendor (non-Claude-family) adversary pass as part of every 3-CLEAN cascade, rather than treating cross-vendor review as an optional/occasional practice — same-model-family adversarial review has systematic blind spots that a same-vendor pass, however many times repeated, does not close. See `cycles/v1.0-brownfield-backfill/lessons.md` for the full codified lesson) |
| S-12.11 | Engine-discipline amendment — mechanical lint/CI gate diffing E-SHD-emitting Display impls against error-taxonomy.md Message Format cells | E-12 | TBD | P2 | [] | [] | draft | [] (draft stub, no BC authored yet; registered 2026-09-10, D-1199, S-25.02 F4 cluster-3 LOCAL adversary pass-9, `[process-gap][codified]` follow-up per the 6+-recurrence rule — the taxonomy Message-Format-cell-≠-shipped-Display drift class has now recurred across `E-SHD-001` (cluster-2 pass-10), `E-SHD-006`/`E-SHD-008`/`E-SHD-009` (cluster-2 pass-8/9), `E-SHD-011`/`E-SHD-012` (cluster-3 pass-8), and `E-SHD-002`/`E-SHD-003`/`E-SHD-011` form (b) (cluster-3 pass-9, this occurrence — 2 named findings F-C3-P9-001/002 plus a companion human-directed exhaustive sweep that caught a third drifted code, `E-SHD-002`). Every prior occurrence was fixed by a single triggering finding naming ONE stale code, with the taxonomy's remaining rows left unexamined until the next pass happened to notice a different code had also drifted. Recommended remedy: a lint hook / CI gate mechanically diffing `grep -oE '"E-SHD-[0-9]+:[^"]*"'` output from every `E-SHD`-emitting Rust source file against `error-taxonomy.md`'s Message Format column, run whenever either side changes (pre-commit on the Rust source, and/or a CI step gating PRs touching either file) — closing the recurrence at its source rather than relying on the next adversary pass to catch the next instance by chance. See `cycles/v1.0-brownfield-backfill/lessons.md` for the full codified lesson `L-BB-D1199-taxonomy-display-drift-recurring-6x-process-gap`) |
| S-12.12 | Deterministic `#[cfg(test)]` fault-injection seam for cluster-3 disk-read-back tests (replaces `spawn_temp_file_corruptor` race) | E-12 | TBD | P3 | [] | [] | draft | [] (draft stub, no BC authored yet; registered 2026-09-10, D-1200, S-25.02 F4 cluster-3 LOCAL adversary pass-10 — FIRST CLEAN PASS, streak 0/3→1/3, 2 LOW non-blocking observations DEFERRED (not fixed in-scope, to keep the remaining 2 passes toward literal 3-CLEAN on the same frozen `8e2a37f4` code). O-C3-P10-001 (LOW, `[process-gap]`): the 3 disk-read-back fault-injection tests (`FC3P6002`/`FC3P7001`/`FC3P8002`, covering the sealed-shard/DANGEROUS-window-heal/happy-path-canonical write-and-read-back sites respectively) each spawn a `spawn_temp_file_corruptor` background thread racing the production write path, bounded 20 retries, hard-failing if the race never lands — correct and non-tautological, but carries a rare spurious-FALSE-FAIL risk on a heavily-loaded CI runner. Recommended remedy: a deterministic `#[cfg(test)]` fault-injection seam, mirroring the existing `FORCE_STAGE_FAILURE` thread-local pattern already used elsewhere in `shard_manager.rs`, that deterministically corrupts the target file at the read-back checkpoint instead of racing a background thread — removes the flakiness risk without weakening coverage. O-C3-P10-002 (LOW, folded into this story's scope, diagnostic-clarity only): a PC4 archival-move failure (`ShardRetentionError::ArchivalMoveFailed`, `E-SHD-002`) is wrapped as `MechanismABackfillError::Io` (`E-SHD-003`) so the `E-SHD-002:` prefix nests inside `E-SHD-003`'s `{source}` slot — defensible under `E-SHD-003`'s documented `<io-error>` placeholder and correct abort-semantics signaling, not a taxonomy-parity violation; recorded here in case this story's fault-injection-seam work also wants an explicit nested-wrap annotation convention. See `s2502-cluster3-local-adversary-pass-10.md` for the full report) |

fail-closed is the correct discipline per OWASP/CWE-636 on unresolved-resource error paths) **+ architect assessment** (the Write-arm `stat()` probe is a NEW cluster-2 backstop, not a real BC-1.18.005 invariant; F-002's fixture was stale/over-broad, never binding on this probe) **+ explicit human approval of refined Option A** (fail-loud `E-SHD-008`/Invariant 8 wins outright; F-002 RETARGETED onto a real, successfully-`stat()`-able 45,000-byte canonical proving its actual invariant — Postcondition 3's content-alone formula ignores `current_shard_bytes`'s value;
